2026-10-25T08:15:31.0056682Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Remove rejected Prefill independent-WG support and serve the attention and MegaMoE candidates with in-job selection

The Prefill A/B experiment was rejected after Firmus kernel bench 40069880580 measured 22–36 percent regressions. Serving never enables this mode. The later padding-WG skip overlay caused the unused image compile gate to fail in build 40173913223, including spills in mode-1-e128 and mode-2-e140.

Remove the Prefill host patch from the ordered host series, its experimental header overlay, both dedicated CPU checkers, their tests and embedded fixtures, and the Dockerfile/build-context stages. Remove Prefill-only host guarding and mode metadata from the kernel benchmark path, and drop the retired candidate aliases from exact-output classification. Refresh the probe README to describe the remaining candidates while preserving the historical measurement record.

Keep the adopted Decode independent-WG host patch, header overlay, compile checker and host provenance guard. Keep all seven serving overlays in their configured order, including the padding-WG skip overlay. Bind the existing host check's baseline hash to the Decode patch input rather than the last overlay position so the later padding overlay does not invalidate the check. Retain the installed JIT header pin; host rebuild records and embedded provenance continue to derive their patch hashes and extension identity from the remaining series.

Validate with Ruff, shell continuation and syntax checks, the complete pytest suite, the actual CPU host selector/descriptor/JIT contract, and CUDA 13.0.88 CPU compilation on the Firmus login node. No GPU jobs, Actions runs or repository history mutations were performed. A fresh image build remains necessary because the host extension changes; full image and serving validation are left to the parent session.

This commit also builds the image for the serving run that e0dc238 meant to
start (its empty build-only commit matched no path filter, and the first
dispatched build stopped at the gate above). The run serves the 8854.80 line
with every new kernel selected at job start: the Decode verify candidate
table (stages, splits, fused FP8 output, last-arriving in-kernel merge,
uneven splits), the fixed single-sequence Prefill FA3 path with register
split, lazy rescale, V byte image, N192 and combinations through the new
Prefill selector, and the MegaMoE padding-WG skip overlay. Profiling is off
because the selectors would push a profiled run past the 17-minute limit.
